# Enable chat conversation creation for unlinked contacts

**Session ID:** ses_2e43ed6bbffeFIG7BeWrWn4BCm
**Created:** 3/23/2026, 2:32:17 PM

## User

Currently if an Contact does not have an linked "ChatwootContact" we show "Nenhum canal de chat disponível para este contato". We need to enable the initiation / creation of a new conversation on the ChatwootInbox that the ChatwootAccount of the logged in user has access.

11: /**
12:  * Channel Picker Modal for Contact "Send Message" action.
13:  *
14:  * Displays available Chatwoot channels (ContactInboxes) for a Contact,
15:  * allowing the user to open an existing conversation or create a new one
16:  * in either a new tab (SSO-authenticated) or the iframe drawer.
17:  *
18:  * Options:
19:  *   - contactId: EspoCRM Contact entity ID
20:  *   - contactName: Contact display name
21:  *   - chatwootAccountEntityId: EspoCRM ChatwootAccount entity ID (for filtering)
22:  */
23: define("chatwoot:views/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker", [
24:     "views/modal",
25: ], function (Dep) {
26:     return Dep.extend({
27:         className: "dialog dialog-record",
28: 
29:         template: "chatwoot:contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker",
30: 
31:         backdrop: true,
32: 
33:         /** @type {Array} Processed channel list for template rendering */
34:         channels: [],
35: 
36:         /** @type {boolean} Whether data is still loading */
37:         isLoading: true,
38: 
39:         /** @type {boolean} Whether an error occurred during loading */
40:         hasError: false,
41: 
42:         /** @type {string|null} Error message to display */
43:         errorMessage: null,
44: 
45:         data: function () {
46:             return {
47:                 isLoading: this.isLoading,
48:                 hasError: this.hasError,
49:                 errorMessage: this.errorMessage,
50:                 hasChannels: this.channels.length > 0,
51:                 channels: this.channels,
52:             };
53:         },
54: 
55:         setup: function () {
56:             this.headerText = this.translate("Select Channel", "labels", "Contact");
57: 
58:             this.buttonList = [
59:                 {
60:                     name: "close",
61:                     label: "Close",
62:                 },
63:             ];
64: 
65:             this.contactId = this.options.contactId;
66:             this.contactName = this.options.contactName;
67:             this.chatwootAccountEntityId = this.options.chatwootAccountEntityId;
68:             this.chatwootAccountId = this.getHelper().getAppParam("chatwootAccountId");
69: 
70:             this.channels = [];
71:             this.isLoading = true;
72:             this.hasError = false;
73:             this.errorMessage = null;
74: 
75:             this._loadData();
76:         },
77: 
78:         /**
79:          * Fetch ChatwootContactInboxes, ChatwootConversations, and
80:          * ChatwootInbox records (for channelType from the linked
81:          * ChatwootInboxIntegration) in parallel, then merge client-side.
82:          */
83:         _loadData: function () {
84:             var contactInboxPromise = Espo.Ajax.getRequest("ChatwootContactInbox", {
85:                 where: [
86:                     {
87:                         type: "equals",
88:                         attribute: "contactId",
89:                         value: this.contactId,
90:                     },
91:                     {
92:                         type: "equals",
93:                         attribute: "chatwootAccountId",
94:                         value: this.chatwootAccountEntityId,
95:                     },
96:                 ],
97:                 select: "id,inboxName,inboxChannelType,chatwootInboxId,inboxId",
98:                 maxSize: 200,
99:             });
100: 
101:             var conversationPromise = Espo.Ajax.getRequest("ChatwootConversation", {
102:                 where: [
103:                     {
104:                         type: "equals",
105:                         attribute: "contactId",
106:                         value: this.contactId,
107:                     },
108:                     {
109:                         type: "equals",
110:                         attribute: "chatwootAccountId",
111:                         value: this.chatwootAccountEntityId,
112:                     },
113:                 ],
114:                 orderBy: "lastActivityAt",
115:                 order: "desc",
116:                 select: "id,chatwootConversationId,chatwootAccountIdExternal,contactInboxId",
117:                 maxSize: 200,
118:             });
119: 
120:             var chatwootInboxPromise = Espo.Ajax.getRequest("ChatwootInbox", {
121:                 where: [
122:                     {
123:                         type: "equals",
124:                         attribute: "chatwootAccountId",
125:                         value: this.chatwootAccountEntityId,
126:                     },
127:                 ],
128:                 select: "id,channelType",
129:                 maxSize: 200,
130:             });
131: 
132:             Promise.all([contactInboxPromise, conversationPromise, chatwootInboxPromise])
133:                 .then(
134:                     function (results) {
135:                         var contactInboxes = (results[0] && results[0].list) || [];
136:                         var conversations = (results[1] && results[1].list) || [];
137:                         var chatwootInboxes = (results[2] && results[2].list) || [];
138: 
139:                         this._processData(contactInboxes, conversations, chatwootInboxes);
140: 
141:                         this.isLoading = false;
142:                         this.reRender();
143:                     }.bind(this)
144:                 )
145:                 .catch(
146:                     function (xhr) {
147:                         this.isLoading = false;
148:                         this.hasError = true;
149: 
150:                         if (xhr && xhr.status === 403) {
151:                             this.errorMessage = "Insufficient permissions";
152:                         } else {
153:                             this.errorMessage = "Failed to load channels";
154:                         }
155: 
156:                         this.reRender();
157:                     }.bind(this)
158:                 );
159:         },
160: 
161:         /**
162:          * Process raw data into the channels array used by the template.
163:          *
164:          * @param {Array} contactInboxes - ChatwootContactInbox records
165:          * @param {Array} conversations - ChatwootConversation records (desc by lastActivityAt)
166:          * @param {Array} chatwootInboxes - ChatwootInbox records (with channelType from ChatwootInboxIntegration)
167:          */
168:         _processData: function (contactInboxes, conversations, chatwootInboxes) {
169:             // Group conversations by contactInboxId — first one is the most recent
170:             var conversationMap = {};
171:             conversations.forEach(function (conv) {
172:                 var key = conv.contactInboxId;
173:                 if (key && !conversationMap[key]) {
174:                     conversationMap[key] = conv;
175:                 }
176:             });
177: 
178:             // Map ChatwootInbox by id for channelType lookup
179:             var inboxMap = {};
180:             chatwootInboxes.forEach(function (cInbox) {
181:                 inboxMap[cInbox.id] = cInbox;
182:             });
183: 
184:             this.channels = contactInboxes.map(
185:                 function (contactInbox) {
186:                     var conversation = conversationMap[contactInbox.id] || null;
187: 
188:                     // Resolve channelType from linked ChatwootInbox
189:                     // (which is a foreign field from ChatwootInboxIntegration)
190:                     var chatwootInbox = contactInbox.inboxId ? inboxMap[contactInbox.inboxId] : null;
191:                     var integrationChannelType = chatwootInbox ? chatwootInbox.channelType : null;
192: 
193:                     var iconInfo = this._getChannelIcon(integrationChannelType);
194: 
195:                     return {
196:                         contactInboxId: contactInbox.id,
197:                         inboxName: contactInbox.inboxName || "Unknown Channel",
198:                         inboxChannelType: contactInbox.inboxChannelType,
199:                         channelTypeLabel: iconInfo.label,
200:                         svgIconUrl: iconInfo.svgIconUrl,
201:                         iconClass: iconInfo.iconClass,
202:                         hasConversation: !!conversation,
203:                         chatwootConversationId: conversation
204:                             ? conversation.chatwootConversationId
205:                             : null,
206:                         chatwootAccountIdExternal: conversation
207:                             ? conversation.chatwootAccountIdExternal
208:                             : null,
209:                         conversationEntityId: conversation
210:                             ? conversation.id
211:                             : null,
212:                     };
213:                 }.bind(this)
214:             );
215:         },
216: 
217:         /**
218:          * Get the icon for a channel using the ChatwootInboxIntegration channelType.
219:          *
220:          * Uses the same resolution logic as the svg-icon-enum field view
221:          * on the ChatwootInbox sidepanel:
222:          *   1. svgIconByValue exact match (e.g. "whatsappQrcode" → "whatsapp")
223:          *   2. pickByContains fallback (e.g. value contains "whatsapp" → "whatsapp")
224:          *   3. Font Awesome fallback for unknown types
225:          *
226:          * @param {string|null} channelType - ChatwootInboxIntegration.channelType (e.g. "whatsappQrcode", "whatsappCloudApi")
227:          * @returns {{ svgIconUrl: string|null, iconClass: string|null, label: string }}
228:          */
229:         _getChannelIcon: function (channelType) {
230:             // Exact value map — mirrors entityDefs ChatwootInbox.channelType.svgIconByValue
231:             var svgIconByValue = {
232:                 whatsappQrcode: "whatsapp",
233:                 whatsappCloudApi: "whatsapp",
234:             };
235: 
236:             // Contains-based fallback — mirrors svg-icon-enum.js pickByContains
237:             var containsMap = {
238:                 whatsapp: "whatsapp",
239:                 waha: "whatsapp",
240:                 telegram: "telegram",
241:                 instagram: "instagram",
242:                 facebook: "messenger",
243:                 messenger: "messenger",
244:             };
245: 
246:             // Icon name → SVG file — mirrors SVG_ICON_FILE_MAP in svg-icon-enum.js
247:             var svgFileMap = {
248:                 whatsapp: "whatsapp.svg",
249:                 telegram: "telegram.svg",
250:                 instagram: "instagram.svg",
251:                 messenger: "messenger.svg",
252:             };
253: 
254:             // Font Awesome fallback for channels without SVG icons
255:             var faIconMap = {
256:                 email: "fas fa-envelope",
257:                 web: "fas fa-globe",
258:                 sms: "fas fa-sms",
259:                 api: "fas fa-plug",
260:                 default: "fas fa-comment",
261:             };
262: 
263:             var value = channelType || "";
264:             var iconName = null;
265: 
266:             // Step 1: Exact match (svgIconByValue)
267:             iconName = svgIconByValue[value] || null;
268: 
269:             // Step 2: Contains-based fallback
270:             if (!iconName && value) {
271:                 var normalized = value.toLowerCase();
272:                 for (var needle in containsMap) {
273:                     if (normalized.includes(needle)) {
274:                         iconName = containsMap[needle];
275:                         break;
276:                     }
277:                 }
278:             }
279: 
280:             // Step 3: Resolve SVG file
281:             var svgFile = iconName ? svgFileMap[iconName] : null;
282: 
283:             if (svgFile) {
284:                 return {
285:                     svgIconUrl: this.getBasePath() + "client/custom/modules/global/res/icons/" + svgFile,
286:                     iconClass: null,
287:                     label: iconName,
288:                 };
289:             }
290: 
291:             // Step 4: Font Awesome fallback
292:             var label = value ? value.toLowerCase() : "default";
293: 
294:             return {
295:                 svgIconUrl: null,
296:                 iconClass: faIconMap[label] || faIconMap["default"],
297:                 label: label,
298:             };
299:         },
300: 
301:         /**
302:          * Get a channel object from the channels array by its template index.
303:          */
304:         _getChannelByIndex: function (index) {
305:             return this.channels[index] || null;
306:         },
307: 
308:         /**
309:          * Build the cwPath for a Chatwoot conversation URL.
310:          */
311:         _buildConversationPath: function (chatwootAccountId, chatwootConversationId) {
312:             return (
313:                 "/app/accounts/" +
314:                 chatwootAccountId +
315:                 "/inbox-view/conversation/" +
316:                 chatwootConversationId
317:             );
318:         },
319: 
320:         /**
321:          * Open a conversation in a new tab using the SSO-authenticated
322:          * #Chatwoot?cwPath= route.
323:          */
324:         _openInNewTab: function (chatwootAccountId, chatwootConversationId) {
325:             var cwPath = this._buildConversationPath(
326:                 chatwootAccountId,
327:                 chatwootConversationId
328:             );
329:             window.open("#Chatwoot?cwPath=" + cwPath, "_blank");
330:         },
331: 
332:         /**
333:          * Open a conversation in the iframe drawer modal.
334:          * Follows the kanban-item.js actionQuickView() pattern.
335:          */
336:         _openInDrawer: function (
337:             chatwootConversationId,
338:             chatwootAccountIdExternal,
339:             conversationEntityId
340:         ) {
341:             this.createView(
342:                 "conversationDrawer",
343:                 "chatwoot:views/chatwoot-conversation/modals/conversation-drawer",
344:                 {
345:                     chatwootConversationId: chatwootConversationId,
346:                     chatwootAccountIdExternal: chatwootAccountIdExternal,
347:                     contactName: this.contactName,
348:                     recordId: conversationEntityId,
349:                 },
350:                 function (view) {
351:                     view.render();
352:                 }
353:             );
354:         },
355: 
356:         /**
357:          * Create a new conversation for a ContactInbox, then invoke the callback
358:          * with the created conversation data.
359:          *
360:          * Shows a loading spinner on the channel row during creation.
361:          */
362:         _createConversation: function (index, callback) {
363:             var channel = this._getChannelByIndex(index);
364:             if (!channel) return;
365: 
366:             // Show loading state on the row
367:             var $row = this.$el.find('.channel-item[data-index="' + index + '"]');
368:             $row.addClass("is-creating");
369: 
370:             Espo.Ajax.postRequest(
371:                 "ChatwootContactInbox/" + channel.contactInboxId + "/createConversation"
372:             )
373:                 .then(
374:                     function (response) {
375:                         // Update the channel data with the new conversation
376:                         channel.hasConversation = true;
377:                         channel.chatwootConversationId =
378:                             response.chatwootConversationId;
379:                         channel.chatwootAccountIdExternal =
380:                             response.chatwootAccountIdExternal;
381:                         channel.conversationEntityId = response.id;
382: 
383:                         $row.removeClass("is-creating");
384: 
385:                         if (callback) {
386:                             callback(response);
387:                         }
388:                     }.bind(this)
389:                 )
390:                 .catch(
391:                     function (xhr) {
392:                         $row.removeClass("is-creating");
393: 
394:                         var errorMsg = this.translate(
395:                             "Failed to create conversation",
396:                             "labels",
397:                             "Contact"
398:                         );
399:                         if (xhr && xhr.responseJSON && xhr.responseJSON.message) {
400:                             errorMsg = xhr.responseJSON.message;
401:                         }
402: 
403:                         Espo.Ui.error(errorMsg);
404:                     }.bind(this)
405:                 );
406:         },
407: 
408:         /**
409:          * Handle "Open in New Tab" action.
410:          * If no conversation exists, creates one first.
411:          */
412:         actionOpenTab: function (data) {
413:             var index = parseInt(data.index, 10);
414:             var channel = this._getChannelByIndex(index);
415:             if (!channel) return;
416: 
417:             if (channel.hasConversation) {
418:                 this._openInNewTab(
419:                     channel.chatwootAccountIdExternal || this.chatwootAccountId,
420:                     channel.chatwootConversationId
421:                 );
422:                 this.close();
423:             } else {
424:                 this._createConversation(
425:                     index,
426:                     function (response) {
427:                         this._openInNewTab(
428:                             response.chatwootAccountIdExternal || this.chatwootAccountId,
429:                             response.chatwootConversationId
430:                         );
431:                         this.close();
432:                     }.bind(this)
433:                 );
434:             }
435:         },
436: 
437:         /**
438:          * Handle "Open in Drawer" action.
439:          * If no conversation exists, creates one first.
440:          */
441:         actionOpenDrawer: function (data) {
442:             var index = parseInt(data.index, 10);
443:             var channel = this._getChannelByIndex(index);
444:             if (!channel) return;
445: 
446:             if (channel.hasConversation) {
447:                 this.close();
448:                 this._openInDrawer(
449:                     channel.chatwootConversationId,
450:                     channel.chatwootAccountIdExternal || this.chatwootAccountId,
451:                     channel.conversationEntityId
452:                 );
453:             } else {
454:                 this._createConversation(
455:                     index,
456:                     function (response) {
457:                         this.close();
458:                         this._openInDrawer(
459:                             response.chatwootConversationId,
460:                             response.chatwootAccountIdExternal || this.chatwootAccountId,
461:                             response.id
462:                         );
463:                     }.bind(this)
464:                 );
465:             }
466:         },
467: 
468:         actionClose: function () {
469:             this.close();
470:         },
471:     });
472: });

(End of file - total 472 lines)
</content>

141: <div class="channel-picker-modal">
142:     {{#if isLoading}}
143:     <div class="loading-state">
144:         <div class="spinner">
145:             <span class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin fa-2x"></span>

147:         <div>{{translate 'Loading...' scope='Global'}}</div>
148:     </div>
149:     {{else}}
150:     {{#if hasError}}

152:         <i class="fas fa-exclamation-triangle"></i>
153:         <div>{{errorMessage}}</div>
154:     </div>
155:     {{else}}
156:     {{#if hasChannels}}
157:     <ul class="channel-list">
158:         {{#each channels}}
159:         <li class="channel-item" data-index="{{@index}}">
160:             <div class="channel-icon">
161:                 {{#if svgIconUrl}}
162:                 <img src="{{svgIconUrl}}" alt="" width="18" height="18">
163:                 {{else}}
164:                 <i class="{{iconClass}}"></i>
165:                 {{/if}}
166:             </div>
167:             <div class="channel-info">
168:                 <div class="channel-name">{{inboxName}}</div>
169:                 <div class="channel-type">{{channelTypeLabel}}</div>
170:             </div>
171:             <div class="channel-actions">
172:                 {{#if hasConversation}}
173:                 <button class="btn btn-default btn-sm action" data-action="openTab" data-index="{{@index}}" title="{{translate 'Open in New Tab' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}">
174:                     <i class="fas fa-external-link-alt"></i> {{translate 'Open in New Tab' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}
175:                 </button>
176:                 <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm action" data-action="openDrawer" data-index="{{@index}}" title="{{translate 'Open in Drawer' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}">
177:                     <i class="fas fa-comments"></i> {{translate 'Open in Drawer' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}
178:                 </button>
179:                 {{else}}
180:                 <button class="btn btn-default btn-sm action" data-action="openTab" data-index="{{@index}}" title="{{translate 'Start in New Tab' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}">
181:                     <i class="fas fa-external-link-alt"></i> {{translate 'Start in New Tab' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}
182:                 </button>
183:                 <button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm action" data-action="openDrawer" data-index="{{@index}}" title="{{translate 'Start Chat' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}">
184:                     <i class="fas fa-plus-circle"></i> {{translate 'Start Chat' category='labels' scope='Contact'}}
185:                 </button>
186:                 {{/if}}

11: /**
12:  * Detail action handler for Contact.
13:  *
14:  * Provides "Send Message" button that opens a channel picker modal
15:  * for sending messages via Chatwoot channels linked to the contact.
16:  */
17: define('chatwoot:handlers/contact/detail-actions', [], function () {
18: 
19:     return class {
20:         constructor(view) {
21:             this.view = view;
22:         }
23: 
24:         /**
25:          * Send Message is available when the current user has a chatSsoUrl
26:          * (meaning they have a linked ChatwootUser with valid SSO).
27:          * This is synchronous — no API call needed.
28:          */
29:         isSendMessageAvailable() {
30:             return !!this.view.getHelper().getAppParam('chatSsoUrl');
31:         }
32: 
33:         /**
34:          * Opens the channel picker modal for sending messages.
35:          *
36:          * @param {Object} data - data-* attributes from the DOM element
37:          * @param {Event} event - the DOM click event
38:          */
39:         sendMessage(data, event) {
40:             const chatwootAccountEntityId = this.view.getHelper().getAppParam('chatwootAccountEntityId');
41: 
42:             if (!chatwootAccountEntityId) {
43:                 Espo.Ui.error(
44:                     this.view.translate('No channels available', 'labels', 'Contact')
45:                 );
46:                 return;
47:             }
48: 
49:             this.view.createView(
50:                 'sendMessageChannelPicker',
51:                 'chatwoot:views/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker',
52:                 {
53:                     contactId: this.view.model.id,
54:                     contactName: this.view.model.get('name'),
55:                     chatwootAccountEntityId: chatwootAccountEntityId,
56:                 },
57:                 (view) => {
58:                     view.render();
59:                 }
60:             );
61:         }
62:     };
63: });

97:     private function executeRequest(

122:             curl_setopt($ch, CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS, $payload);
123:         }
124:

546:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['message'];

548:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['error'];
549:             }
550:             
551:             $this->log->error('Chatwoot API Error (getUserLoginUrl): ' . json_encode($response));
552:             throw new Error($errorMsg);
553:         }
554: 
555:         if (!isset($response['body']['url'])) {
556:             throw new Error('Chatwoot API response missing login URL.');
557:         }
558: 
559:         return $response['body']['url'];
560:     }

562:     // ========================================================================
563:     // Contact API Methods (Account-level API)

566:     /**
567:      * Create a contact on Chatwoot via Account API.
568:      *
569:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform
570:      * @param string $accountApiKey The account-level API key
571:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
572:      * @param array<string, mixed> $contactData Contact data to send
573:      * @return array<string, mixed> Response data from Chatwoot API
574:      * @throws Error
575:      */
576:     public function createContact(
577:         string $platformUrl,
578:         string $accountApiKey,
579:         int $accountId,
580:         array $contactData
581:     ): array {
582:         // Validate required field
583:         if (!isset($contactData['inbox_id'])) {
584:             throw new Error('inbox_id is required to create a contact in Chatwoot.');
585:         }
586:         
587:         $url = rtrim($platformUrl, '/') . '/api/v1/accounts/' . $accountId . '/contacts';
588:         
589:         $payload = json_encode($contactData);
590:         
591:         if ($payload === false) {
592:             throw new Error('Failed to encode contact data to JSON.');
593:         }
594: 
595:         $headers = [
596:             'api_access_token: ' . $accountApiKey,
597:             'Content-Type: application/json',
598:             'Content-Length: ' . strlen($payload)
599:         ];
600: 
601:         $response = $this->executeRequest($url, 'POST', $payload, $headers);
602: 
603:         if ($response['code'] < 200 || $response['code'] >= 300) {
604:             $errorMsg = 'Chatwoot API error: HTTP ' . $response['code'];
605:             
606:             if (isset($response['body']['message'])) {
607:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['message'];
608:             } elseif (isset($response['body']['error'])) {
609:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['error'];
610:             }
611:             
612:             $this->log->error('Chatwoot API Error (createContact): ' . json_encode($response));
613:             throw new Error($errorMsg);
614:         }
615: 
616:         return $response['body'];
617:     }
618: 
619:     /**
620:      * Update a contact on Chatwoot via Account API.
621:      *
622:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform
623:      * @param string $accountApiKey The account-level API key
624:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
625:      * @param int $contactId The Chatwoot contact ID
626:      * @param array<string, mixed> $contactData Contact data to update
627:      * @return array<string, mixed> Response data from Chatwoot API
628:      * @throws Error
629:      */
630:     public function updateContact(
631:         string $platformUrl,
632:         string $accountApiKey,
633:         int $accountId,
634:         int $contactId,
635:         array $contactData
636:     ): array {
637:         $url = rtrim($platformUrl, '/') . '/api/v1/accounts/' . $accountId . '/contacts/' . $contactId;
638:         
639:         $payload = json_encode($contactData);
640:         
641:         if ($payload === false) {
642:             throw new Error('Failed to encode contact data to JSON.');
643:         }
644: 
645:         $headers = [
646:             'api_access_token: ' . $accountApiKey,
647:             'Content-Type: application/json',
648:             'Content-Length: ' . strlen($payload)
649:         ];
650: 
651:         $response = $this->executeRequest($url, 'PATCH', $payload, $headers);
652: 
653:         if ($response['code'] < 200 || $response['code'] >= 300) {
654:             $errorMsg = 'Chatwoot API error: HTTP ' . $response['code'];
655:             
656:             if (isset($response['body']['message'])) {
657:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['message'];
658:             } elseif (isset($response['body']['error'])) {
659:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['error'];
660:             }
661:             
662:             $this->log->error('Chatwoot API Error (updateContact): ' . json_encode($response));
663:             throw new Error($errorMsg);
664:         }
665: 
666:         return $response['body'];
667:     }

669:     /**
670:      * Get contact details from Chatwoot via Account API.

672:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform

674:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
675:      * @param int $contactId The Chatwoot contact ID
676:      * @return array<string, mixed> Contact data
677:      * @throws Error
678:      */
679:     public function getContact(
680:         string $platformUrl,
681:         string $accountApiKey,
682:         int $accountId,

687:         $headers = [
688:             'api_access_token: ' . $accountApiKey,
689:             'Content-Type: application/json'
690:         ];
691: 
692:         $response = $this->executeRequest($url, 'GET', null, $headers);
693: 
694:         if ($response['code'] < 200 || $response['code'] >= 300) {
695:             $errorMsg = 'Failed to get contact from Chatwoot: HTTP ' . $response['code'];
696:

710:     /**
711:      * Search for contacts on Chatwoot via Account API.
712:      *
713:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform
714:      * @param string $accountApiKey The account-level API key
715:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
716:      * @param string $query Search query (email, phone, or name)
717:      * @return array<string, mixed> Search results with 'payload' containing contacts
718:      * @throws Error
719:      */
720:     public function searchContacts(
721:         string $platformUrl,
722:         string $accountApiKey,
723:         int $accountId,
724:         string $query
725:     ): array {
726:         $url = rtrim($platformUrl, '/') . '/api/v1/accounts/' . $accountId . '/contacts/search';
727:         $url .= '?q=' . urlencode($query);
728:         
729:         $headers = [
730:             'api_access_token: ' . $accountApiKey,
731:             'Content-Type: application/json'
732:         ];
733: 
734:         $response = $this->executeRequest($url, 'GET', null, $headers);
735: 
736:         if ($response['code'] < 200 || $response['code'] >= 300) {
737:             $errorMsg = 'Failed to search contacts on Chatwoot: HTTP ' . $response['code'];
738:             
739:             if (isset($response['body']['message'])) {
740:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['message'];
741:             } elseif (isset($response['body']['error'])) {
742:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['error'];
743:             }
744:             
745:             $this->log->error('Chatwoot API Error (searchContacts): ' . json_encode($response));
746:             throw new Error($errorMsg);
747:         }
748: 
749:         return $response['body'];
750:     }
751: 
752:     /**
753:      * Search for a contact by phone number.
754:      *
755:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform
756:      * @param string $accountApiKey The account-level API key
757:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
758:      * @param string $phoneNumber The phone number to search for
759:      * @return array|null Contact data if found, null otherwise
760:      * @throws Error
761:      */
762:     public function searchContactByPhone(
763:         string $platformUrl,
764:         string $accountApiKey,
765:         int $accountId,
766:         string $phoneNumber
767:     ): ?array {
768:         $results = $this->searchContacts($platformUrl, $accountApiKey, $accountId, $phoneNumber);
769:         
770:         // Check different response structures
771:         $contacts = [];
772:         if (isset($results['payload'])) {
773:             $contacts = $results['payload'];
774:         } elseif (isset($results['contacts'])) {
775:             $contacts = $results['contacts'];
776:         } elseif (is_array($results) && isset($results[0])) {
777:             $contacts = $results;
778:         }
779:         
780:         // Find exact phone number match
781:         foreach ($contacts as $contact) {
782:             if (isset($contact['phone_number']) && $contact['phone_number'] === $phoneNumber) {
783:                 return $contact;
784:             }
785:         }
786:         
787:         // If no exact match, return first result if it exists
788:         return !empty($contacts) ? $contacts[0] : null;
789:     }

973:     /**
974:      * Delete a team from a Chatwoot account.
975:      *
976:      * @param string $platformUrl The base URL of the Chatwoot platform
977:      * @param string $accountApiKey The account-level API key
978:      * @param int $accountId The Chatwoot account ID
979:      * @param int $teamId The Chatwoot team ID
980:      * @return void
981:      * @throws Error
982:      */
983:     public function deleteTeam(
984:         string $platformUrl,
985:         string $accountApiKey,
986:         int $accountId,
987:         int $teamId
988:     ): void {
989:         $url = rtrim($platformUrl, '/') . '/api/v1/accounts/' . $accountId . '/teams/' . $teamId;
990:         
991:         $headers = [
992:             'api_access_token: ' . $accountApiKey,
993:             'Content-Type: application/json'
994:         ];
995: 
996:         $response = $this->executeRequest($url, 'DELETE', null, $headers);
997: 
998:         // Accept 204 No Content, 200 OK, or 404 (already deleted) as success
999:         if ($response['code'] !== 200 && $response['code'] !== 204 && $response['code'] !== 404) {
1000:             $errorMsg = 'Failed to delete team from Chatwoot: HTTP ' . $response['code'];
1001:             
1002:             if (isset($response['body']['message'])) {
1003:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['message'];
1004:             } elseif (isset($response['body']['error'])) {
1005:                 $errorMsg .= ' - ' . $response['body']['error'];
1006:             }
1007:             
1008:             $this->log->error('Chatwoot API Error (deleteTeam): ' . json_encode($response));
1009:             throw new Error($errorMsg);
1010:         }
1011:         
1012:         // Log if team was already deleted
1013:         if ($response['code'] === 404) {
1014:             $this->log->info("Team $teamId was already deleted from account $accountId or doesn't exist");
1015:         }
1016:     }

93:         $chatwootContactId = $contactInbox->get('chatwootContactId');
94:         if (!$chatwootContactId) {
95:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootContactInbox has no linked ChatwootContact.");
96:         }
97: 
98:         $chatwootContact = $entityManager->getEntityById('ChatwootContact', $chatwootContactId);
99:         if (!$chatwootContact) {
100:             throw new BadRequest("Linked ChatwootContact not found.");
101:         }
102: 
103:         $externalContactId = (int) $chatwootContact->get('chatwootContactId');
104:         if (!$externalContactId) {
105:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootContact has no external chatwootContactId.");
106:         }
107: 
108:         // ChatwootInboxId (external int, directly on the entity)
109:         $externalInboxId = (int) $contactInbox->get('chatwootInboxId');
110:         if (!$externalInboxId) {
111:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootContactInbox has no chatwootInboxId.");
112:         }
113: 
114:         // ChatwootAccount → apiKey, chatwootAccountId (external int), platformId → ChatwootPlatform → backendUrl
115:         $chatwootAccount = $entityManager->getEntityById('ChatwootAccount', $inboxAccountId);
116:         if (!$chatwootAccount) {
117:             throw new BadRequest("Linked ChatwootAccount not found.");
118:         }
119: 
120:         $accountApiKey = $chatwootAccount->get('apiKey');
121:         if (!$accountApiKey) {
122:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootAccount has no API key.");
123:         }
124: 
125:         $externalAccountId = (int) $chatwootAccount->get('chatwootAccountId');
126:         if (!$externalAccountId) {
127:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootAccount has no external chatwootAccountId.");
128:         }
129: 
130:         $platformId = $chatwootAccount->get('platformId');
131:         if (!$platformId) {
132:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootAccount has no linked platform.");
133:         }
134: 
135:         $platform = $entityManager->getEntityById('ChatwootPlatform', $platformId);
136:         if (!$platform) {
137:             throw new BadRequest("Linked ChatwootPlatform not found.");
138:         }
139: 
140:         $platformUrl = $platform->get('backendUrl');
141:         if (!$platformUrl) {
142:             throw new BadRequest("ChatwootPlatform has no backendUrl.");
143:         }
144: 
145:         // === Call Chatwoot API to create conversation ===
146:         $apiClient = $this->injectableFactory->create(ChatwootApiClient::class);
147: 
148:         $chatwootResponse = $apiClient->createConversation(
149:             $platformUrl,
150:             $accountApiKey,
151:             $externalAccountId,
152:             $externalContactId,
153:             $externalInboxId
154:         );
155: 
156:         // Use display_id preferentially for URL-facing conversation number (Decision #16)
157:         $chatwootConversationId = $chatwootResponse['display_id'] ?? $chatwootResponse['id'] ?? null;
158: 
159:         if (!$chatwootConversationId) {
160:             throw new Error("Chatwoot API response missing conversation ID.");
161:         }
162: 
163:         // === Create local ChatwootConversation entity (Decision #15) ===
164: 
165:         // Resolve contact name for the conversation name
166:         $contactEntityId = $contactInbox->get('contactId');
167:         $contactName = '';
168:         if ($contactEntityId) {
169:             $contact = $entityManager->getEntityById('Contact', $contactEntityId);
170:             if ($contact) {
171:                 $contactName = $contact->get('name') ?? '';
172:             }
173:         }
174: 
175:         $name = date('Y-m-d') . ($contactName ? ' - ' . $contactName : '');
176: 
177:         // Resolve teams: try from contactInbox first, fall back to account
178:         $teamsIds = $contactInbox->getLinkMultipleIdList('teams');
179:         if (empty($teamsIds)) {
180:             $teamsIds = $chatwootAccount->getLinkMultipleIdList('teams');
181:         }
182: 
183:         $data = [
184:             'name' => $name,
185:             'chatwootConversationId' => (int) $chatwootConversationId,
186:             'chatwootAccountId' => $inboxAccountId,
187:             'chatwootContactId' => $chatwootContactId,
188:             'contactId' => $contactEntityId,
189:             'contactInboxId' => $id,
190:             'chatwootInboxId' => $externalInboxId,
191:             'inboxName' => $contactInbox->get('inboxName'),
192:             'inboxChannelType' => $contactInbox->get('inboxChannelType'),
193:             'status' => 'open',
194:         ];
195: 
196:         if (!empty($teamsIds)) {
197:             $data['teamsIds'] = $teamsIds;
198:         }
199: 
200:         // Use silent option to suppress hooks/notifications (Decision #19)
201:         $conversationEntity = $entityManager->createEntity('ChatwootConversation', $data, ['silent' => true]);
202: 
203:         // Return data needed by the client to open the conversation
204:         $result = new stdClass();
205:         $result->chatwootConversationId = (int) $chatwootConversationId;
206:         $result->chatwootAccountIdExternal = $externalAccountId;
207:         $result->id = $conversationEntity->getId();
208: 
209:         return $result;
210:     }
211: }

30: 
31: /**
32:  * AppParam that provides the EspoCRM entity ID of the user's ChatwootAccount.
33:  *
34:  * Resolution path:
35:  *   EspoCRM User → ChatwootUser (via assignedUserId) → ChatwootAccountUserMembership → chatwootAccountId (EspoCRM entity FK)
36:  *
37:  * This is needed for filtering ChatwootContactInboxes by the `chatwootAccount` link FK.
38:  * Returned as part of the /api/v1/App/user response.
39:  */
40: class ChatwootAccountEntityId implements AppParam
41: {
42:     public function __construct(
43:         private User $user,
44:         private EntityManager $entityManager,
45:         private Log $log
46:     ) {}
47: 
48:     /**
49:      * Get the EspoCRM entity ID of the ChatwootAccount for the current user.
50:      *
51:      * @return string|null The EspoCRM entity ID or null if user has no Chatwoot account
52:      */
53:     public function get(): ?string
54:     {
55:         try {
56:             $userId = $this->user->getId();
57:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountEntityId: Getting account entity ID for user {$userId}");
58: 
59:             // Step 1: Find ChatwootUser assigned to this EspoCRM user.
60:             // Deterministic ordering (oldest first) for multi-platform stability.
61:             $chatwootUser = $this->entityManager
62:                 ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootUser')
63:                 ->where(['assignedUserId' => $userId])
64:                 ->order('createdAt', 'ASC')
65:                 ->findOne();
66: 
67:             if (!$chatwootUser) {
68:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountEntityId: No ChatwootUser found for user {$userId}");
69:                 return null;
70:             }
71: 
72:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountEntityId: Found ChatwootUser: " . $chatwootUser->getId());
73: 
74:             // Step 2: Find membership for this ChatwootUser.
75:             // Deterministic ordering (oldest first) for multi-account stability.
76:             $membership = $this->entityManager
77:                 ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootAccountUserMembership')
78:                 ->where(['chatwootUserId' => $chatwootUser->getId()])
79:                 ->order('createdAt', 'ASC')
80:                 ->findOne();
81: 
82:             if ($membership) {
83:                 // Step 3: Return the EspoCRM entity FK (not the external int).
84:                 $accountEntityId = $membership->get('chatwootAccountId');
85:                 if ($accountEntityId) {
86:                     $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountEntityId: Found account entity ID: {$accountEntityId}");
87:                     return $accountEntityId;
88:                 }
89:             }
90: 
91:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountEntityId: No membership found for ChatwootUser " . $chatwootUser->getId());
92:             return null;
93:         } catch (\Exception $e) {
94:             $this->log->error(
95:                 'ChatwootAccountEntityId: Failed to get account entity ID for user ' . $this->user->getId() . ': ' . $e->getMessage()
96:             );
97:             return null;
98:         }
99:     }
100: }

2380:     public function sendTemplateMessage(

24: namespace Espo\Modules\Chatwoot\Classes\AppParams;
25: 
26: use Espo\Entities\User;
27: use Espo\ORM\EntityManager;
28: use Espo\Tools\App\AppParam;
29: use Espo\Core\Utils\Log;
30: 
31: /**
32:  * AppParam that provides the Chatwoot Account ID for the current user.
33:  *
34:  * Resolution path:
35:  *   EspoCRM User → ChatwootUser (via assignedUserId) → ChatwootAccountUserMembership → ChatwootAccount → chatwootAccountId (external int)
36:  *
37:  * This is returned as part of the /api/v1/App/user response.
38:  */
39: class ChatwootAccountId implements AppParam
40: {
41:     public function __construct(
42:         private User $user,
43:         private EntityManager $entityManager,
44:         private Log $log
45:     ) {}
46: 
47:     /**
48:      * Get the Chatwoot Account ID for the current user.
49:      *
50:      * @return int|null The Chatwoot account ID or null if user has no Chatwoot account
51:      */
52:     public function get(): ?int
53:     {
54:         try {
55:             $userId = $this->user->getId();
56:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: Getting account ID for user {$userId}");
57: 
58:             // Step 1: Find ChatwootUser assigned to this EspoCRM user.
59:             // Deterministic ordering (oldest first) for multi-platform stability (Decision #13).
60:             $chatwootUser = $this->entityManager
61:                 ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootUser')
62:                 ->where(['assignedUserId' => $userId])
63:                 ->order('createdAt', 'ASC')
64:                 ->findOne();
65: 
66:             if (!$chatwootUser) {
67:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: No ChatwootUser found for user {$userId}");
68:                 return null;
69:             }
70: 
71:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: Found ChatwootUser: " . $chatwootUser->getId());
72: 
73:             // Step 2: Find membership for this ChatwootUser.
74:             // Deterministic ordering (oldest first) for multi-account stability (Decision #8, #13).
75:             $membership = $this->entityManager
76:                 ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootAccountUserMembership')
77:                 ->where(['chatwootUserId' => $chatwootUser->getId()])
78:                 ->order('createdAt', 'ASC')
79:                 ->findOne();
80: 
81:             if ($membership) {
82:                 // Step 3: Resolve external account ID from membership's account link.
83:                 $accountEntityId = $membership->get('chatwootAccountId');
84:                 if ($accountEntityId) {
85:                     return $this->resolveExternalAccountId($accountEntityId);
86:                 }
87:             }
88: 
89:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: No membership found for ChatwootUser " . $chatwootUser->getId());
90:             return null;
91:         } catch (\Exception $e) {
92:             $this->log->error(
93:                 'ChatwootAccountId: Failed to get account ID for user ' . $this->user->getId() . ': ' . $e->getMessage()
94:             );
95:             return null;
96:         }
97:     }
98: 
99:     /**
100:      * Resolve the Chatwoot API account ID from a ChatwootAccount entity ID.
101:      */
102:     private function resolveExternalAccountId(string $entityId): ?int
103:     {
104:         $account = $this->entityManager->getEntityById('ChatwootAccount', $entityId);
105:         if (!$account) {
106:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: ChatwootAccount not found: {$entityId}");
107:             return null;
108:         }
109: 
110:         $chatwootAccountId = $account->get('chatwootAccountId');
111:         if (!$chatwootAccountId) {
112:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: Account has no chatwootAccountId");
113:             return null;
114:         }
115: 
116:         $this->log->debug("ChatwootAccountId: Found chatwootAccountId: {$chatwootAccountId}");
117:         return $chatwootAccountId;
118:     }
119: }

32: /**
33:  * AppParam that provides the Chatwoot SSO login URL for the current user.
34:  *
35:  * Resolution path (Phase 5):
36:  *   EspoCRM User → ChatwootUser (via assignedUserId) → ChatwootPlatform → SSO URL
37:  *
38:  * This is returned as part of the /api/v1/App/user response.
39:  */
40: class ChatwootSsoUrl implements AppParam
41: {
42:     public function __construct(
43:         private User $user,
44:         private EntityManager $entityManager,
45:         private ChatwootApiClient $apiClient,
46:         private Log $log
47:     ) {}
48: 
49:     /**
50:      * Get the Chatwoot SSO URL for the current user.
51:      *
52:      * @return string|null The SSO login URL or null if user has no Chatwoot account
53:      */
54:     public function get(): ?string
55:     {
56:         try {
57:             $userId = $this->user->getId();
58:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: Getting SSO URL for user {$userId}");
59: 
60:             // Single-path lookup: ChatwootUser assigned to this EspoCRM user.
61:             // Deterministic ordering (oldest first) for multi-platform stability (Decision #13).
62:             $chatwootUser = $this->entityManager
63:                 ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootUser')
64:                 ->where(['assignedUserId' => $userId])
65:                 ->order('createdAt', 'ASC')
66:                 ->findOne();
67: 
68:             if (!$chatwootUser) {
69:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: No ChatwootUser found for user {$userId}");
70:                 return null;
71:             }
72: 
73:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: Found ChatwootUser: " . $chatwootUser->getId());
74: 
75:             // Check if user has been synced with Chatwoot
76:             $chatwootUserId = $chatwootUser->get('chatwootUserId');
77:             if (!$chatwootUserId) {
78:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: ChatwootUser has no chatwootUserId");
79:                 return null;
80:             }
81: 
82:             // Get platform directly from ChatwootUser (it has a direct link to platform)
83:             $platformId = $chatwootUser->get('platformId');
84:             if (!$platformId) {
85:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: ChatwootUser has no platformId");
86:                 return null;
87:             }
88: 
89:             $platform = $this->entityManager->getEntityById('ChatwootPlatform', $platformId);
90:             if (!$platform) {
91:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: ChatwootPlatform not found: {$platformId}");
92:                 return null;
93:             }
94: 
95:             // Get platform URL and access token
96:             $platformUrl = $platform->get('backendUrl');
97:             $accessToken = $platform->get('accessToken');
98: 
99:             if (!$platformUrl || !$accessToken) {
100:                 $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: Platform missing URL or access token");
101:                 return null;
102:             }
103: 
104:             // Get SSO login URL from Chatwoot API
105:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: Fetching login URL from Chatwoot API for user {$chatwootUserId}");
106:             $ssoUrl = $this->apiClient->getUserLoginUrl($platformUrl, $accessToken, $chatwootUserId);
107:             $this->log->debug("ChatwootSsoUrl: Successfully got SSO URL: {$ssoUrl}");
108: 
109:             return $ssoUrl;
110:         } catch (\Exception $e) {
111:             $this->log->error(
112:                 'ChatwootSsoUrl: Failed to get SSO URL for user ' . $this->user->getId() . ': ' . $e->getMessage()
113:             );
114:             return null;
115:         }
116:     }
117: }

## Research Summary

The CRM uses EspoCRM as its framework with a custom `Chatwoot` module that integrates deeply with a Chatwoot messaging platform. When viewing a Contact detail page, there's a "Send Message" button (defined in `clientDefs/Contact.json`) that opens a channel picker modal. This modal currently fetches `ChatwootContactInbox` records linked to the Contact, showing available channels. If the Contact has **no linked ChatwootContact** (and therefore no `ChatwootContactInbox` records), the modal shows the empty state message: "Nenhum canal de chat disponível para este contato" ("No chat channels available for this contact"). The user's request is to enable conversation initiation even when the Contact has no pre-existing ChatwootContact link, by allowing the user to select from the ChatwootInboxes available on the ChatwootAccount they're a member of.

## Existing Architecture & Patterns

- **AppParams pattern**: The logged-in user's Chatwoot context is resolved at login via `AppParam` classes and available client-side via `this.getHelper().getAppParam(...)`. The key params are: `chatwootAccountEntityId` (EspoCRM FK for ChatwootAccount), `chatwootAccountId` (external Chatwoot int), `chatSsoUrl`, `chatwootFrontendUrl`. (Reference: `custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Classes/AppParams/ChatwootAccountEntityId.php`)

- **User→Account resolution**: EspoCRM User → ChatwootUser (via `assignedUserId`) → ChatwootAccountUserMembership → ChatwootAccount. The membership also has a `chatwootInboxes` many-to-many link, indicating which inboxes the user has access to. (Reference: `custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootAccountUserMembership.json`)

- **Channel picker modal**: The `send-message-channel-picker` JS view fetches `ChatwootContactInbox`, `ChatwootConversation`, and `ChatwootInbox` records in parallel, merging them client-side into a `channels` array rendered in the template. When `channels` is empty, the `{{else}}` branch renders the "No channels available" empty state. (Reference: `client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/views/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker.js`)

- **Conversation creation via ContactInbox**: The existing `POST ChatwootContactInbox/:id/createConversation` endpoint creates a conversation in Chatwoot and a local `ChatwootConversation` entity. It requires a pre-existing `ChatwootContactInbox` with linked `ChatwootContact`. (Reference: `custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Controllers/ChatwootContactInbox.php`)

- **Chatwoot API for contact+conversation creation**: `ChatwootApiClient::createContact()` creates a contact in Chatwoot (requires `inbox_id`), and `findOrCreateContact()` searches by phone then creates if not found. `createConversation()` creates a conversation given `contact_id` + `inbox_id`. (Reference: `custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Services/ChatwootApiClient.php` lines 576, 2268, 2316)

- **Contact detail actions handler**: Checks `chatSsoUrl` for visibility, and resolves `chatwootAccountEntityId` from AppParams to pass to the modal. (Reference: `client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/handlers/contact/detail-actions.js`)

## Key Files & Components Found

- **`client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/views/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker.js`**: The modal that displays channels. Currently only loads `ChatwootContactInbox` records. This is the primary file that needs modification to also show available inboxes when no channels exist.

- **`client/custom/modules/chatwoot/res/templates/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker.tpl`**: The Handlebars template for the modal. The `{{#if hasChannels}}` / `{{else}}` empty state section needs to be enhanced to show inbox selection.

- **`client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/handlers/contact/detail-actions.js`**: The handler that opens the modal. May need to pass additional data (like phone number) to the modal.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Controllers/ChatwootContactInbox.php`**: The `createConversation` backend action. A new endpoint or enhancement is needed to handle the case where there's no `ChatwootContactInbox` yet (create contact → create contact inbox → create conversation).

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Services/ChatwootApiClient.php`**: Contains `createContact()`, `findOrCreateContact()`, `createConversation()` methods that will be used by the new backend action.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/routes.json`**: Route definitions. A new route will need to be registered here.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/i18n/pt_BR/Contact.json`** and **`en_US/Contact.json`**: Translation files that already contain relevant labels like "Start Chat", "Creating conversation...", etc. New labels may be needed (e.g., "Select inbox to start conversation").

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootInbox.json`**: ChatwootInbox entity - has `chatwootAccountId`, `chatwootInboxId`, `name`, `channelType` (foreign from integration). The `accountUserMemberships` link shows which users have access.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootAccountUserMembership.json`**: Contains the `chatwootInboxes` linkMultiple field (many-to-many with `ChatwootInbox`), which defines which inboxes a user can access.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Classes/AppParams/ChatwootAccountEntityId.php`**: Resolves the user's ChatwootAccount entity ID. Used by the frontend to filter queries.

- **`custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Jobs/SyncConversationsFromChatwoot.php`**: Contains `createContactInboxFromConversation()` method (line 555) showing how to create a `ChatwootContactInbox` entity locally.

## Technical Constraints & Considerations

- **Chatwoot API requires `inbox_id` to create a contact**: When creating a contact in Chatwoot, you must specify which inbox. The created contact gets a `contact_inbox` junction linking them to that inbox. This means the user must pick an inbox before the contact can be created.

- **Phone number is needed for WhatsApp inboxes**: The `findOrCreateContact` pattern uses phone number to search for existing contacts. The EspoCRM Contact entity has a `phoneNumber` field of type `phone` that can be used.

- **Access control via membership inboxes**: ChatwootAccountUserMembership has a `chatwootInboxes` many-to-many relationship. The user should only see inboxes they have access to. The frontend can query `ChatwootInbox` filtered by `chatwootAccountId` and the API's access control should handle inbox filtering, OR the frontend can also query the user's membership's inboxes explicitly.

- **Existing `ChatwootContactInbox` creation pattern**: The sync job (`SyncConversationsFromChatwoot`) shows how to create a local `ChatwootContactInbox` entity after a contact inbox is created on Chatwoot. A new backend endpoint must replicate this pattern.

- **The `ChatwootContact` might already exist**: A Contact might have a linked `ChatwootContact` (synced via Chatwoot) but no `ChatwootContactInbox` for a specific inbox. The flow needs to handle: (a) no ChatwootContact at all, (b) ChatwootContact exists but no ContactInbox for the selected inbox.

- **ChatwootContact link to EspoCRM Contact**: ChatwootContact has a `contact` link (belongsTo Contact). When creating a new ChatwootContact, this link must be established.

- **Team assignment**: New entities (ChatwootContact, ChatwootContactInbox, ChatwootConversation) need `teamsIds` set, following the pattern in the existing `createConversation` controller.

- **`deleteId` soft-delete pattern**: ChatwootContact, ChatwootContactInbox, and ChatwootConversation use the `deleteId` field for soft-deletion with unique constraints. New records must use `deleteId = "0"`.

## Unanswered Questions

- **Should the new flow create both a ChatwootContact AND ChatwootContactInbox simultaneously?** The Chatwoot `createContact` API with an `inbox_id` creates both the contact and the contact_inbox in one call, so this should be a single backend operation.

- **What identifier should be used for non-WhatsApp inboxes?** For WhatsApp, the phone number is the natural identifier. For email inboxes, the email would be used. For web/API channels, it's unclear what identifier to use. Should we restrict this feature to WhatsApp/email inboxes only, or support all types?

- **Should all inboxes from the user's ChatwootAccount be shown, or only those linked to the user's membership (via `chatwootInboxes` on `ChatwootAccountUserMembership`)?** The membership has a `chatwootInboxes` many-to-many relationship that could filter visibility.

- **Should the modal also show existing channels (ContactInboxes) alongside the new "initiate on inbox" option?** Or should the new inbox selection only appear when the contact has zero existing channels?

## Research Results: ChatwootAccountUserMembership -> chatwootInboxes Many-to-Many Relationship

### 1. Junction Table Name and Structure

**Entity definition file:**
`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootAccountUserMembership.json`

**Junction table name:** `ChatwootAccountUserMembershipChatwootInbox`

(In EspoCRM's ORM convention this maps to database table `chatwoot_account_user_membership_chatwoot_inbox` with snake_case conversion.)

**Link definition from the membership side** (lines 194-199):
```json
"chatwootInboxes": {
    "type": "hasMany",
    "entity": "ChatwootInbox",
    "relationName": "chatwootAccountUserMembershipChatwootInbox",
    "foreign": "accountUserMemberships"
}
```

**Inverse link from the ChatwootInbox side** (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootInbox.json`, lines 135-140):
```json
"accountUserMemberships": {
    "type": "hasMany",
    "entity": "ChatwootAccountUserMembership",
    "relationName": "chatwootAccountUserMembershipChatwootInbox",
    "foreign": "chatwootInboxes"
}
```

**Junction table columns** (inferred from EspoCRM `hasMany`+`relationName` convention and the cascadeDelete declarations):
| Column | Source |
|---|---|
| `id` | Auto PK |
| `chatwootAccountUserMembershipId` | FK to `ChatwootAccountUserMembership.id` (entityDefs line 226) |
| `chatwootInboxId` | FK to `ChatwootInbox.id` (ChatwootInbox.json line 154) |
| `deleted` | Soft-delete flag (EspoCRM standard) |

**Cascade delete** is registered from both sides:
- Membership side: `{"table": "ChatwootAccountUserMembershipChatwootInbox", "column": "chatwootAccountUserMembershipId"}` (line 226)
- Inbox side: `{"table": "ChatwootAccountUserMembershipChatwootInbox", "column": "chatwootInboxId"}` (ChatwootInbox.json line 154)

**Field declaration** (line 142-143):
```json
"chatwootInboxes": {
    "type": "linkMultiple"
}
```

---

### 2. How Membership Inboxes Are Queried in Existing Code

#### a) **InboxAccessResolver** (the central mechanism)
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Tools/Acl/InboxAccessResolver.php`

This is the **authoritative pattern** for querying a user's inboxes via memberships. The resolution chain is:
```
User -> ChatwootUser.assignedUserId -> ChatwootAccountUserMembership -> chatwootInboxes
```

Key code (lines 47-64):
```php
$membershipCollection = $this->entityManager
    ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootAccountUserMembership')
    ->leftJoin('chatwootUser')
    ->where([
        'chatwootUser.assignedUserId' => $userId,
    ])
    ->find();

$inboxIdMap = [];

foreach ($membershipCollection as $membership) {
    if (!$membership instanceof CoreEntity) {
        continue;
    }
    foreach ($membership->getLinkMultipleIdList('chatwootInboxes') as $inboxId) {
        $inboxIdMap[$inboxId] = true;
    }
}
```

**Important:** This uses `getLinkMultipleIdList('chatwootInboxes')` -- an N+1 pattern that issues a separate query per membership to load its linked inboxes. It caches results per user ID in `$allowedInboxIdListByUserId`.

Admins get `null` (unrestricted), non-admins get a concrete `string[]` allow-list.

#### b) **SyncInboxMembersFromChatwoot** (the sync job that populates the junction table)
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Jobs/SyncInboxMembersFromChatwoot.php`

This job is the **write side** -- it reconciles the junction table by:
1. Fetching inbox members from the Chatwoot API
2. Resolving each member to a `ChatwootAccountUserMembership` via `resolveMembershipByPlatformUserId()`
3. Reconciling the `accountUserMemberships` relation on the inbox (lines 286-320):

```php
// Add new membership links
$this->entityManager
    ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootInbox')
    ->getRelation($inbox, 'accountUserMemberships')
    ->relateById($membershipId, null, ['skipHooks' => true]);

// Remove old membership links
$this->entityManager
    ->getRDBRepository('ChatwootInbox')
    ->getRelation($inbox, 'accountUserMemberships')
    ->unrelateById($membershipId, ['skipHooks' => true]);
```

Note: It operates from the **inbox side** (`accountUserMemberships`), not the membership side.

#### c) **ValidateInboxRelation** hook
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Hooks/ChatwootAccountUserMembership/ValidateInboxRelation.php`

An `afterRelate` hook that enforces an **account boundary** -- a membership can only be linked to inboxes belonging to the same `chatwootAccountId`. If mismatched, it immediately unlinks and throws a `BadRequest`.

#### d) **Frontend channel-picker** (read-only query from JS)
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/views/contact/modals/send-message-channel-picker.js`

The frontend does **not** directly query the junction table. Instead, it fetches `ChatwootInbox` records filtered by `chatwootAccountId` (line 120-130) and uses the EspoCRM API's built-in access control (which uses the `ByMembershipInbox` filter under the hood) to scope results.

### 3. How the Frontend Resolves the Logged-in User's Membership ID

**The frontend does NOT currently resolve the membership ID as an AppParam.**

What it **does** resolve:

#### a) **AppParams delivered on login** (`/api/v1/App/user` response):
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/app/appParams.json`
```json
{
    "chatSsoUrl":             "...ChatwootSsoUrl",
    "chatwootAccountId":      "...ChatwootAccountId",       // External Chatwoot int ID
    "chatwootFrontendUrl":    "...ChatwootFrontendUrl",
    "chatwootAccountEntityId":"...ChatwootAccountEntityId"   // EspoCRM FK of ChatwootAccount
}
```

The `ChatwootAccountEntityId` AppParam (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Classes/AppParams/ChatwootAccountEntityId.php`) resolves the chain:
```
User -> ChatwootUser (assignedUserId) -> ChatwootAccountUserMembership -> chatwootAccountId
```
But it **returns the account ID, not the membership ID itself**.

#### b) **Frontend usage of AppParams:**
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/handlers/contact/detail-actions.js` (line 40):
```js
const chatwootAccountEntityId = this.view.getHelper().getAppParam('chatwootAccountEntityId');
```
This is used to pass into the channel picker modal for filtering.

#### c) **Inline resolution in defaults-preparator** (when creating new records):
**File:** `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/client/custom/modules/chatwoot/src/handlers/chatwoot-inbox-integration/defaults-preparator.js` (lines 36-76):

The InboxIntegration defaults preparator does a **two-step API query** to resolve the membership (ChatwootUser -> ChatwootAccountUserMembership), but only uses it to extract `chatwootAccountId` and `chatwootAccountName` for prefilling the form -- it discards the membership entity itself.

```js
const chatwootUserResponse = await Espo.Ajax.getRequest("ChatwootUser", {
    where: [{ type: "equals", attribute: "assignedUserId", value: userId }],
    maxSize: 1, orderBy: "createdAt", order: "asc",
});
const chatwootUser = chatwootUserResponse.list?.[0];
if (chatwootUser) {
    const membershipResponse = await Espo.Ajax.getRequest("ChatwootAccountUserMembership", {
        where: [{ type: "equals", attribute: "chatwootUserId", value: chatwootUser.id }],
        maxSize: 1, orderBy: "createdAt", order: "asc",
    });
    const membership = membershipResponse.list?.[0];
    if (membership?.chatwootAccountId) {
        return { chatwootAccountId: membership.chatwootAccountId, ... };
    }
}
```

**Summary:** There is **no `chatwootAccountUserMembershipEntityId` AppParam**. The membership ID is never exposed to the frontend as a session-level value. The frontend only knows the `chatwootAccountEntityId` (the account, not the membership).

### 4. Select/Access Control Filters for ChatwootInbox

**There is NO `selectDefs/ChatwootInbox.json` file.** No `aclDefs/ChatwootInbox.json` exists either.

This means **ChatwootInbox has no custom selectDefs access control filter**. When non-admin users list ChatwootInbox records, they rely only on EspoCRM's standard team-based ACL (the `teams` linkMultiple field on ChatwootInbox), not on the membership->inbox relationship.

**However**, the sibling entities DO have inbox-scoped access control:

| Entity | selectDefs file | Access filter |
|---|---|---|
| `ChatwootConversation` | `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/selectDefs/ChatwootConversation.json` | `ByMembershipInbox` for all/onlyTeam/onlyOwn |
| `ChatwootContact` | `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/selectDefs/ChatwootContact.json` | `ByMembershipInbox` for all/onlyTeam/onlyOwn |
| `ChatwootMessage` | `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/selectDefs/ChatwootMessage.json` | `ByMembershipInbox` for all/onlyTeam/onlyOwn |
| **ChatwootInbox** | **None** | **Standard team ACL only** |

All three filters delegate to the same central resolver:
**`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Tools/Acl/InboxAccessResolver.php`**

The `ByMembershipInbox` pattern varies by entity:
- **Conversation**: `WHERE inboxId IN (allowedInboxIdList)` -- direct FK match
- **Contact**: `LEFT JOIN contactInboxes WHERE contactInboxAccess.inboxId IN (...)` -- join through ContactInbox
- **Message**: `LEFT JOIN conversation WHERE conversationAccess.inboxId IN (...)` -- join through Conversation

## Research Findings

### 1. WhatsApp JID / LID Fields on the Contact Entity

**There are NO fields related to WhatsApp JID or WhatsApp LID on the Contact entity.** I searched across all three Contact entityDefs layers and found nothing:

- **Core EspoCRM Contact** (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/application/Espo/Modules/Crm/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/Contact.json`): No jid/lid fields. Standard CRM fields only.

- **Global custom overlay** (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Global/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/Contact.json`): Only adds `cpf`, `dataNascimento`, `sexo` fields. No jid/lid.

- **Chatwoot module overlay** (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/Contact.json`): Only adds `whatsAppOptedOut` (bool) field and link definitions to Chatwoot entities. No jid/lid.

The `whatsappId` field exists only on **ChatwootInboxIntegration** (not Contact), and refers to the WhatsApp account ID of the *inbox itself* (format: `5511999999999@c.us`):

- **File**: `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootInboxIntegration.json`, lines 137-142:
  ```json
  "whatsappId": {
      "type": "varchar",
      "maxLength": 50,
      "readOnly": true,
      "tooltip": true
  }
  ```

A codebase-wide grep for `jid`, `whatsappJid`, `whatsapp_jid`, `whatsappLid`, `whatsapp_lid` in PHP files returned **zero results** in the custom modules.

### 2. Contact Entity Phone Number Field Structure

The Contact `phoneNumber` field uses EspoCRM's built-in `phone` field type.

**File**: `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/application/Espo/Modules/Crm/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/Contact.json`, lines 233-238:
```json
"phoneNumber": {
    "type": "phone",
    "typeList": ["Mobile", "Office", "Home", "Fax", "Other"],
    "defaultType": "Mobile",
    "isPersonalData": true
}
```

The `phone` field type (defined at `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/application/Espo/Resources/metadata/fields/phone.json`) is **NOT a simple varchar column**. It is a complex field backed by a separate `phone_number` table with a many-to-many relationship. Key characteristics:

- **`fieldDefs.notStorable: true`** -- the `phoneNumber` attribute is NOT a direct column on the `contact` table
- **Actual fields**: `isOptedOut`, `isInvalid`, `""` (primary number), `data` (JSON array of all numbers)
- **ORM Converter**: `Espo\Core\Utils\Database\Orm\FieldConverters\Phone` -- manages the join to the `phone_number` table
- **Value Factory**: `Espo\Core\Field\PhoneNumber\PhoneNumberGroupFactory`
- When you call `$contact->get('phoneNumber')`, it returns the **primary** phone number as a string (resolved through a relation join to the `phone_number` table)
- The full data is in `$contact->get('phoneNumberData')` -- an array of `{phoneNumber, type, primary, optOut, invalid}` objects

### 3. How `findOrCreateContact` in ChatwootApiClient.php Resolves Phone Numbers

**File**: `/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Services/ChatwootApiClient.php`, lines 2256-2303:

```php
/**
 * @param string $phoneNumber Normalized phone number (+55...)
 * @return array{id: int, name: string, phone_number: string}
 */
public function findOrCreateContact(
    string $platformUrl,
    string $accountApiKey,
    int $accountId,
    int $inboxId,
    string $phoneNumber,
    ?string $name = null
): array {
    // Try to find existing contact by phone
    $existing = $this->searchContactByPhone($platformUrl, $accountApiKey, $accountId, $phoneNumber);

    if ($existing) {
        return $existing;
    }

    // Create new contact
    $contactData = [
        'inbox_id' => $inboxId,
        'phone_number' => $phoneNumber,
    ];
    if ($name) {
        $contactData['name'] = $name;
    }
    $newContact = $this->createContact($platformUrl, $accountApiKey, $accountId, $contactData);
    // ...
}
```

**Expected format**: E.164 normalized phone number with `+` prefix (e.g., `+5511987654321`).

The `searchContactByPhone` method (lines 762-789) does an **exact string match** on the `phone_number` field from Chatwoot's API response:

```php
// Find exact phone number match
foreach ($contacts as $contact) {
    if (isset($contact['phone_number']) && $contact['phone_number'] === $phoneNumber) {
        return $contact;
    }
}
// If no exact match, return first result if it exists
return !empty($contacts) ? $contacts[0] : null;
```

Key identifier/phone fields:

| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `chatwootContactId` | int (readOnly) | The Chatwoot platform's external contact ID |
| `phoneNumber` | varchar(50) | Phone number synced from Chatwoot's `phone_number` field |
| `identifier` | varchar(255) | Chatwoot's `identifier` field (the contact's channel-specific identifier) |
| `email` | email | Email from Chatwoot |
| `name` | varchar(255) | Name from Chatwoot |
| `contact` | link -> Contact | Link to the EspoCRM Contact entity |

There is a **composite index** on `phoneNumber` + `chatwootAccountId`:

**ChatwootContactInbox entity** (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Resources/metadata/entityDefs/ChatwootContactInbox.json`) has a `sourceId` field:

This `sourceId` comes from Chatwoot's `contact_inboxes[].source_id` -- the per-inbox identifier for a contact. For WhatsApp inboxes, this is typically the phone number or the WhatsApp-specific identifier.

### 5. How WhatsApp Campaign Code Resolves Phone Numbers from Contacts

The flow spans two files:

**Step 1 -- Audience Resolution** in `WhatsAppCampaignService.php` (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Services/WhatsAppCampaignService.php`):

Lines 118, 151 -- reads `phoneNumber` from the EspoCRM Contact entity (the `phone` field type, which returns the primary phone number string):
```php
$phone = $this->normalizePhone($contact->get('phoneNumber'));
```

The `normalizePhone` method (lines 454-498) converts to E.164:
```php
private function normalizePhone(?string $phone): ?string
{
    if (!$phone) return null;

    // Remove all non-digit characters
    $digits = preg_replace('/\D/', '', $phone);

    if (!$digits || strlen($digits) < 10) return null;

    // Brazilian E.164: 55 + 2-digit DDD + 9-digit mobile (13 digits total)
    if (str_starts_with($digits, '55') && strlen($digits) >= 12 && strlen($digits) <= 13) {
        if (strlen($digits) === 12) {
            // Old format with 8-digit mobile needs "9" prefix added
            $ddd = substr($digits, 2, 2);
            $local = substr($digits, 4);
            if (preg_match('/^[6-9]/', $local)) {
                $digits = '55' . $ddd . '9' . $local;
            }
        }
        return '+' . $digits;
    }

    // If 10-11 digits, assume Brazilian local number
    if (strlen($digits) >= 10 && strlen($digits) <= 11) {
        // ... adds +55 prefix, inserts 9 if needed
        return '+55' . $digits;
    }

    // Already has country code (other countries)
    if (strlen($digits) > 11) return '+' . $digits;

    return null;
}
```

**Step 2 -- Junction Record Creation** in `WhatsAppCampaignService.php`, lines 345-354:
```php
foreach ($audience as $item) {
    $this->entityManager->createEntity('WhatsAppCampaignContact', [
        'whatsAppCampaignId' => $campaignId,
        'contactId' => $item['contactId'],
        'chatwootAccountId' => $chatwootAccountId,
        'phoneNumber' => $item['phoneNumber'],  // Already normalized E.164
        'contactName' => $item['contactName'],
        'status' => 'Pending',
    ]);
}
```

**Step 3 -- Sending** in `ProcessWhatsAppCampaignChunk.php` (`/home/antl3x/repos/monostax/mono/components/crm/source/custom/Espo/Modules/Chatwoot/Jobs/ProcessWhatsAppCampaignChunk.php`), lines 233-243:
```php
$phoneNumber = $campaignContact->get('phoneNumber');  // From WhatsAppCampaignContact
$contactName = $campaignContact->get('contactName');

$chatwootContact = $this->chatwootApiClient->findOrCreateContact(
    $ctx['platformUrl'],
    $ctx['accountApiKey'],
    $ctx['chatwootAccountIdExternal'],
    $ctx['inboxId'],
    $phoneNumber,   // E.164 format (+5511987654321)
    $contactName
);
```
